[dcheck-to-check] Convert DCHECKs in ui/ [10/23]

As part of effort to harden Chromium's safety, turn DCHECKs
into CHECKs. The converted CHECKs are not fatal until M163.

See also:
https://docs.google.com/document/d/1tzGQwGG0lJ6K7Ur8wBW6E58CvJc1JQNyiX0ryHe7A5I/edit?usp=sharing

This patch is generated by the following script:
https://crrev.com/c/7849874/18/tools/memory/dcheck/replace_dchecks_with_checks.py

PSA:
https://groups.google.com/a/chromium.org/g/chromium-dev/c/vd1Qt-egELY/m/badCjDnsDAAJ
LSC (Google internal):
http://goto.google.com/chrome-lsc-dcheck-to-check-conversions

fd137a554ce06832200f5fa037b8458addc8456e
parent
7d8d6c5e6e98e4bd197a7d6ec3829717ba036c5d [ diff ] 
mojo: Consistently validate ipcz header sizes

Serialized ipcz driver object headers were validated inconsistently on
deserialization. A DataPipe header size larger than the received data
hit a span bounds CHECK instead of being rejected, and an ObjectHeader
size that wasn't a multiple of 8 misaligned the handle and object data
following it.

Check every header size against its minimum size, the available data and
8-byte alignment, so that such malformed objects are rejected.

Fixed: 580212323

ff758e09523106871c8e554c2fa5c35db3f7ffa2 [ diff ] 
[blink] Migrate OriginTrialContext sets to base::EnumSet

- Define OriginTrialFeatureSet using base::EnumSet and replace the
  three OriginTrialFeature HashSet members and InstallFeatures
  parameter in OriginTrialContext with 16-byte inline bitsets.
- Eliminate PartitionAlloc hash table allocations per ExecutionContext
  and replace hash lookups in feature checks with bitset operations.
- Add unit tests covering worker feature inheritance, navigation
  feature activation, and idempotent feature installation.

f32a99c27fe111724409b6331e51dc4453a14908 [ diff ] 
Share the manifest provider interface

Introduce ManifestProvider and have ManifestMonitorDelegate extend it.
Reuse the provider implementation for desktop manifest registration and
notifications.

Require callers to specify the file checked during installation
verification. Replay a cached manifest directory only to the new
listener, while preserving desktop registration and update priorities.

This also prepares the shared interface and installer policy for Android
component config loading in a follow-up CL.

a52ba64f80e0627a3588dca4153fff7062db13fa [ diff ] 
[Gardener] Disable VTS expand-on-hover FullWebUI tests on Windows

On Windows (win11-arm64-rel-tests and Win10 Tests x64, in addition to
win-asan), the FullWebUI (GetParam() == true) instantiations of
VerticalTabStripExpandOnHoverInteractiveUiTest and
VerticalTabStripExpandOnHoverRtlInteractiveUiTest time out in
ui_test_utils::BringBrowserWindowToFront() during test setup in
InProcessBrowserTest::PreRunTestOnMainThread(), while the Classic
(GetParam() == false) instantiations pass.

Broaden the OmniboxTestParams() guard from BUILDFLAG(IS_WIN) &&
defined(ADDRESS_SANITIZER) to BUILDFLAG(IS_WIN) so the Classic omnibox
tests continue running on Windows while the FullWebUI variants are
skipped.

2bb905a55b0f90d4bc10e4d35d14983dc35651c7 [ diff ] 
ttc: Report CRAS audio effects to DebugSink on ChromeOS

Chrome-wide echo cancellation isn't built on ChromeOS, so TTC always
opens the capture stream with the device parameters from
AudioManagerCras. Those already carry the per-board AEC, NS and AGC
effects and the Finch-gated DSP allowance flags, so CRAS applies them
without any extra request from TTC. Report those effects to DebugSink
so the active processing is visible on ChromeOS.

Align corner and corner-* shorthands with current spec grammar
📋 Metadata
commit
a03933235b204882ac711186bad21798a0f52cb1
[ log ]
author
Helmut Januschka <helmut@januschka.com>

tree
1d38641a10b2d68eaf7d89bb98a611f7ebba450a
parent
cb811338344e6738546b54bb13fe16b4112a3970 [ diff ] 
Align corner and corner-* shorthands with current spec grammar

The initial implementation (crrev.com/c/7747994) parsed the pre-2025-08
prototype grammar: slash-separated per-corner values with a required
shape and a "normal" keyword. The CSSWG replaced it with
"corner: <'corner-shape'> || <'border-radius'>" (and matching side and
single-corner forms); see w3c/csswg-drafts#11623, #13889, and the
order fix in w3c/csswg-drafts#14607.

Either component may now be omitted and resets to its initial value,
the slash belongs to the radius part, and shapes use corner-copy
rules. Serialization emits shape before radius per the grammar's
canonical order, omitting initial components. The upstream WPTs
encoding the old grammar are rewritten to match the current ED.

Still behind the experimental CSSCornersShorthand flag; this does not
re-enable the feature (reverted in crrev.com/c/8486987).

omnibox: Drop surplus shared WebUI popups on hide

cb811338344e6738546b54bb13fe16b4112a3970

tree
491f4db1f4fe3cc217d207a12bf18c19d7cb4ece
parent
0645ffc2c5808477a52f3a4ffd807d5addb9b3b0 [ diff ] 
omnibox: Drop surplus shared WebUI popups on hide

With OmniboxWebUIPopupShareAcrossWindows, a window that needs a popup
while every other window's popup is busy (e.g. shown, or waiting on a
file dialog) creates its own (crrev.com/c/8695085). That copy then stays
until its window closes, so a profile can keep more than one popup of a
kind.

When a popup hides, destroy the idle popups of the same kind in the
profile's other windows and keep the one that just hid, which is in the
window the user is in. A copy that is still busy stays and is dropped on
a later hide. The steady state returns to one popup of each kind. Only
a hide after a real show triggers this, not the hide that puts a new
popup into its resting state. The work runs in a posted task, outside
the hide, which can run from a widget activation change.

Bug: 578985321

tree
196e4e8f2ec1e100c9fb0ce151f7d96c49519f69
parent
07a25a53482f9b14eefca2388c6147625695e5ad [ diff ] 
[GEiC] Do not auto-pin daisy-chained tabs in Gemini Enterprise

When the GEiC side panel opens a new tab via CreateTab (such as from the
three-dot overflow menu's "Send feedback" or "File a bug" items) or
daisy-chains to a new tab, GlicInstanceImpl binds the side panel to the
new tab with pin_on_bind = true. While consumer GiC uses pinnedTabs$
directly as its multi-tab context list, GEiC's BrowserContextService
manages explicit single-tab attachment and leaves the new tab unattached
("+ Add this tab"), causing the Chrome tab strip to show the blue shared
underline while the side panel indicates the tab is not shared.

Gate auto-pinning on daisy-chained tabs (in CreateTab and
MaybeDaisyChainToTab) to non-Gemini-Enterprise providers so GEiC only
pins tabs when explicitly attached by the side panel.

Screencast: https://screencast.googleplex.com/cast/NDU4OTY0NDYxNTI1NDAxNnw5NzFmOTYyNy0yZA
Screenshot (After): https://screenshot.googleplex.com/KSaff4up8sgbwqU

0b1b2e703fd902956f897c2ac0cb808328a656e6 [ diff ] 
Revert CHECK_LE to DCHECK_LE in AXRange::Iterator::operator++

In AXRange::Iterator::operator++, the CHECK_LE macro was converted from
DCHECK_LE during a bulk conversion. This CHECK_LE can fail and cause a
crash in production.

This CL reverts the CHECK_LE back to DCHECK_LE and adds a TODO comment
to re-evaluate converting it back to a CHECK when appropriate.

This fix was generated by the Crash Autofix pipeline for crash report
crash/a3bd068c08400000. Please see go/crash-autofix for more details.

7fd467c5169c949272e3704b12742c1c9b2b55aa
parent
009f4a9ca32f241a91662a1ff8b7194879f3e171 [ diff ] 
[base] Skip Dispatcher's PA observer hooks for memory tools

For AsanRawPtrServiceV2, https://crrev.com/c/8367005 enabled
PartitionAlloc observer hooks when MEMORY_TOOL_REPLACES_ALLOCATOR=1.
AsanRawPtrServiceV2 replaces these observer hooks in Configure().

The previous hooks were registered by //components/memory_system via
Dispatcher at ChromeMainDelegate::CommonEarlyInitialization(). However,
replacing them does not affect Dispatcher's behavior because prior to
https://crrev.com/c/8367005, Dispatcher's hooks were never invoked when
memory tools replaced the allocator.

To explicitly preserve this existing behavior, guard Dispatcher's
SetObserverHooks() with !PA_BUILDFLAG(MEMORY_TOOL_REPLACES_ALLOCATOR).

9bb9619df89943553c5331a7cf947f51c5755e3f [ diff ] 
Add base class for common CSP reporting steps to outside context

A frame receives a CSP violation detected by its frame host through
LocalFrame.ReportContentSecurityPolicyViolation(), and a worker/worklet
creator receives a violation detected while fetching the module graph
through OutsideContextCSPViolationReporter. Both share the same
reporting steps on the context that holds the policy.

Add OutsideContextCSPViolationReporterBase that implements
OutsideContextCSPViolationReporter.ReportCSPViolation() with the
shared steps, and let both LocalFrameMojoHandler and
OutsideSettingsCSPViolationReporter derive from the base class to
provide the parts that differ.

LocalFrame.ReportContentSecurityPolicyViolation() is removed, and
replaced by OutsideContextCSPViolationReporter, which is associated
with LocalFrame to keep the same message ordering as the removed
method.

8a1c9c768b6a82e1d25101c9b45e7f8fe1911465
parent
380f39be870aae440622047ff80519bb7afcc010 [ diff ] 
[iframe] Defer responsive resize to lifecycle

This patch defers responsive resize of `<iframe>`s to
lifecycle, from synchronously running forced layout.

The natural-size measurement and change notification are done
in the rendering loop when `target_state == kPaintClean`,
which is the same criteria as the `ResizeObserver`.

They run before `UpdateStyleAndLayoutIfNeededRecursive()`,
because natural-size should be measured with the [ICB lock],
which can set `NeedsLayout()`. Running it first can keep child
layouts to at most two per frame, instead of three if running
it after.

Tests that read `offsetWidth` or `offsetHeight` synchronously
inside `load` listeners are updated to wait for a rendering
frame.

Changes are under a runtime flag as a kill switch.

[ICB lock]: https://drafts.csswg.org/css-sizing-4/#locked-embedded-icb-size

feedback: Add Feedback.ChromeOSApp.ScreenshotAndTabInfo histogram

An upcoming fix will update the feedback screenshot checkbox to
explicitly mention tab titles. To ensure this doesn't reduce
screenshot opt-ins, we need a reliable baseline metric.

The existing IncludedScreenshot histogram is insufficient because it
cannot distinguish between "screenshot unavailable" and "user
unchecked the box", nor does it track open-tab info.

This CL introduces Feedback.ChromeOSApp.ScreenshotAndTabInfo to track
the inclusion state of screenshots and tab info per sent report.
Landing this first allows us to compare the upcoming UI changes
against a baseline.

Bug: b:274072859
Test: browser_tests --gtest_filter=ChromeOsFeedbackDelegateTest.RecordsScreenshotAndTabInfoHistogram

- Declarations without definitions, such as
  ReplaceSelectionCommand::HandleStyleSpans() and
  IsLegacyAppleHTMLSpanElement().

  (whose generated protocol headers in sources include

ash: Use ash-side pref name constants instead of chrome/common ones

Several files under //chrome/browser/ash and //chrome/browser/ui/ash
use pref names from chrome/common/pref_names.h even though an ash-side
mirror with the same value already exists in
ash/constants/chrome_pref_names.h. Switch them to ash::chrome_prefs:
- kDownloadDefaultDirectory (ash/preferences/preferences.cc)
- kDevToolsAvailability (kiosk_troubleshooting_tools_browsertest.cc)
- kForceEphemeralProfiles (chrome_shimless_rma_delegate_unittest.cc)
- kAudioCaptureAllowed (capture_mode_browsertest.cc)

Also, prefs::kEasyUnlockAllowed ("easy_unlock.allowed") is only used by
smart_lock_service_unittest.cc. The pref is actually registered and
mapped to the EasyUnlockAllowed policy via
ash::multidevice_setup::kSmartLockAllowedPrefName, which has the same
value. Use it in the test and delete the duplicated constant from
chrome/common/pref_names.h. The pref itself is still in use, so no
migration is needed.

Remove the now-unneeded includes of chrome/common/pref_names.h and the
corresponding DEPS exceptions.

parent

indigo: Add Snooze IPH for context menu discovery

When a user who is not cued about Try on You via Contextual Cueing
visits an eligible shopping page, show a Snooze IPH anchored to the top
of the browser window to help them discover the context menu entry
point. Users who can be cued via Contextual Cueing are excluded so that
the same user does not see both a proactive anchored message and this
promo. Engaging with Try on You from any entry point notifies User
Education that the feature was used, closing any active promo and
suppressing future impressions.

The promo is attempted at most once per eligible page load once the tab
is visible, and is aborted if the tab becomes hidden or navigates away
to an ineligible page. It is disabled by default until the triggering
details are finalized.

📝 Commit Message (First Line)
Report CSP violations of worker/worklet module fetches on the creator
📋 Metadata
commit
f342565ae6632200939938d2dce2f243240e80f0
[ log ]
author
Byungwoo Lee <blee@igalia.com>
Thu Nov 05 05:14:31 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Thu Nov 05 05:14:31 2026
tree
57109752a94270c0f605ad098b7c69e3fec1f816
parent
81d6ae19d7d9ce46a3a7a559685eec12dfd9c1cb [ diff ] 
Report CSP violations of worker/worklet module fetches on the creator

A worker's or worklet's module graph is fetched with the outside
settings object (the creator's settings) as the request's client. Per
the spec, violations detected while fetching the module graph belong
to the request's client's global object, so they need to be reported
on the context that created the worker or worklet [1][2].

The commit 0a81d78ca88a5b0207e6a24a6f45eb5d455e693e dispatched the CSP
violation event on the creator through per-type WorkerReportingProxy
plumbing, but that approach only covered dispatching the event, not
the rest of the reporting such as posting reports, the console message,
and the inspector issue.

This CL replaces the plumbing with a mojo interface
OutsideContextCSPViolationReporter. The worker/worklet creator binds
the receiver (OutsideSettingsCSPViolationReporter) and passes the
remote to the worker/worklet, so that OutsideSettingsCSPDelegate sends
the violation information to the creator through the reporter.

The new approach covers SharedWorker as well as DedicatedWorker and
worklets, which the previous commit covered. For SharedWorker, the
mojo remote is passed only for the connection that creates the worker,
so only the creator context receives the violations.

When the creator receives the violation, it runs the whole reporting
steps: logging the console message, dispatching the event, posting the
report, and adding the inspector issue.

This is behind the experimental runtime flag:
- DispatchWorkerCSPViolationEventForModuleGraphFetching

[1] https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-csp/#create-violation-for-request
[2] https://html.spec.whatwg.org/C/#fetch-a-module-worker-script-tree

DISABLE_SPELLCHECKER: Intended test domain

[crubit] Stop passing `-D_LIBCPP_DISABLE_VISIBILITY_ANNOTATIONS`.

Before this CL, `rust_api_from_cpp` passed
`-D_LIBCPP_DISABLE_VISIBILITY_ANNOTATIONS` to `rs_bindings_from_cc`.
Without this define, Crubit did not generate bindings for items in
`namespace std`, because libc++ puts the `type_visibility` attribute on
this namespace.

The current Crubit version (27b7bf8db8465c0109ee5ef4a5cbdf595e16856d)
accepts default visibility attributes on namespaces (upstream commit
347dc44d2).  After this CL, `rust_api_from_cpp` does not pass the
define.

[ttc] Add TtcDebugSession

Adds TtcDebugSession, the in-memory debug record of one TTC session for
the upcoming debug tools. It implements DebugSink and keeps pipeline
counters, a timeline of events (server frames labelled by their content,
e.g. "transcript:input") and the exact audio sent and received, via
TtcSessionAudioRecorder. Observers get batched updates every 100 ms that
only cover what happened after they started observing, and ToValue()
exports everything but the audio for bug reports.

Memory is bounded: labels are truncated and the timeline is capped.
Nothing creates a session yet.

Session errors come in through OnError(), which isn't part of DebugSink:
they're reported by SessionControllerImpl, which will hold the concrete
forwarder, not by the pipeline code DebugSink exists for.

Part 5 of the chrome://ttc-internals series. Design: go/ttc-internals

b7bc4fd8819f7b645414619e8af463ac586a44ab
parent
84d7fa1672446428954b5a2a77075f5fb8ce63e9 [ diff ] 
siso: avoid O(N^2) string concatenation in ar.star

In `build/config/siso/ar.star`, `__ar_create` (used by the
`lld_thin_archive` handler when `remote-link` or `default-remote` is
enabled) builds the `//` extended filename table and the archive member
header section via repeated immutable Starlark string concatenation
(`content += ...` and `data += __file_header(...)`), copying O(N^2)
bytes across N archive members.

Accumulate `content_lines` and `data` into lists and join them once via
`"".join(...)`. Across the top 20 largest Chromium `.a` targets (17,321
`.o` inputs with `--config=remote-link`), this reduces cumulative step
`duration_nanos` by 4.1x (3,319ms -> 802ms) and handler `run_nanos` by
5.4x (2,763ms -> 514ms), with `obj/v8/libwee8.a` (2,581 members)
dropping from 313ms to 51ms `run_nanos` (6.2x faster) and 20/20 SHA-256
output parity.

Bug: None

📋 Metadata

c0b5eb6e30fe68476c5601c8a7789b29dd36eab2
[ log ]

tree
6f1d05e7b4debfff753a4581895bf3e9b600fe80
parent
538a1972ef4913134a442d76978db33d1cd5b3df [ diff ] 
AddToHomescreenCoordinator: Adopt JniUniquePtr

AddToHomescreenCoordinator stores its native
::webapps::AddToHomescreenCoordinator pointer in raw long field
mNativeCoordinator. Passing raw integer handles across the JNI boundary
bypasses type checking and smart pointer lifecycle enforcement, risking
use-after-free bugs if the native object is accessed after destruction.

Migrate mNativeCoordinator to JniUniquePtr with a package-private
JniTypeToken mapped to ::webapps::AddToHomescreenCoordinator via
@JniType. Return jni_zero::MakeUnique from the C++ factory, destroy the
owned instance via JniUniquePtr.destroy(), and track mFlowCompleted so
synchronous completion inside startForAppMenu() destroys the returned
pointer immediately.

Open VirtualDocumentPath.writeFile streams with explicit "wt" mode
📋 Metadata
commit
8e34aee45c47efdf14a45a39357214679ddc9f42
[ log ]
author
Keigo Oka <oka@google.com>
Thu Nov 05 04:27:28 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Thu Nov 05 04:27:28 2026
tree
9ce683e69038f28ad5450e23240392a178b68293
parent
c3383081e3025a65e5540528044f5a8f001e3943 [ diff ] 
Open VirtualDocumentPath.writeFile streams with explicit "wt" mode

VirtualDocumentPath.writeFile() promises that an existing document's
content is truncated before writing, but opened its stream with the
one-argument ContentResolver.openOutputStream(uri). That overload passes
mode "w", whose truncation behavior varies across DocumentsProvider
implementations. It also logged the target URI on failure.

Pass "wt" explicitly to ContentResolver.openOutputStream(targetUri, "wt")
so document providers truncate existing content before writing, and log
a static message on failure without the URI.

d8a05c9df7edd4413e6abe9b3865c11f659c4b47
parent
7fba7b136c4d53544f78ac772d3a8ed7ee4e84ed [ diff ] 
Revert empty filter CHECK back to DCHECK in ExecuteSaveFile

When ExecuteSaveFile is called with an empty filter, the CHECK condition
triggers a crash. This converts the CHECK back to a DCHECK and annotates
it with a TODO comment as per the crash fix guidelines, preventing
crashes in release builds when an empty filter is passed.

This fix was generated by the Crash Autofix pipeline for crash report
crash/757feb0f08400000. Please see go/crash-autofix for more details.

6f7095464b317e2c4ed90d053969a6eca156fde0 [ diff ] 
[cc] Remove trilinear filtering layer plumbing

Nothing sets trilinear filtering on cc::Layers anymore now that the
ui::Layer requests are gone. Remove cc::Layer::SetTrilinearFiltering(),
the EffectNode bit, the kTrilinearFiltering render surface reason and
the corresponding field in the viz layer context mojom. cc no longer
asks for mipmaps on render passes; the viz-side generate_mipmap support
is left in place.

Implement candidate-based positioning for the prompt box.

Updates the prompt box positioning logic to evaluate a prioritized list
of candidate positions (below, above, beside, inside the selection, or
fallback to window corner) based on available viewport space and reading
direction (LTR/RTL).

Pre-allocates space for contextual suggestions so the prompt box does
not jump when suggestions load, and introduces max-width constraints
allowing inline fulfillment cards to shrink to fit down to 320px.

[webapps] Activate blink::Manifest Mojo typemap and drop bridges [16/N]

Complete the blink::Manifest validating value class migration by
activating the Mojo C++ typemap and removing all temporary conversion
bridges:

- Implement mojo::StructTraits<blink::mojom::ManifestDataView,
  ::blink::Manifest> in manifest_mojom_traits.{h,cc} with all 40 field
  getters and a Read() method returning base::expected<void,
  mojo::DeserializationError> that enforces intrinsic manifest
  invariants, logs WebApp.Manifest.InvariantViolation to UMA, and
  attaches the invariant error code via
  DeserializationError::CustomCode.
- Fix a dangling std::u16string_view in ConvertAndTruncateOptionalString
  by returning std::optional<std::u16string> by value.
- Register blink.mojom.Manifest (with default_constructible = false) and
  its 8 newly typemapped nested structs in cpp_typemaps in
  //third_party/blink/public/mojom/BUILD.gn.
- Update content::ManifestManagerHost to receive blink::Manifest and
  std::optional<blink::Manifest> directly from Mojo IPCs, retaining only
  contextual checks and MaybeOverrideManifest.
- Delete temporary bridges ManifestFromMojom and ManifestToMojom,
  blink::IsEmptyManifest, and legacy mojom::Manifest overloads of
  blink::IsDefaultManifest.
- Add full 40-field round-trip and invariant violation deserialization
  unit tests in manifest_mojom_traits_unittest.cc and clean up obsolete
  intrinsic invariant tests in manifest_browsertest.cc.

NO_IFTTT=Only changing comments in the impacted area.

tree
8c9e96dc9f149917bbf2faad851358342b4de698
parent
b5d3f44bf0e6d0c27afe2f9b7c3a26f4d825d170 [ diff ] 
[FSA] Pass BindingContext to FileSystemAccessHandleBase helper

Whether or not the context is "third-party" is a function of _more_ than
just the StorageKey, since SharedWorkers created via
`StorageAccessHandle::BindSharedWorker` use first-party StorageKeys
alongside a tag enum which restricts their cookie access (see
`SharedWorkerInstance::DoesRequireCrossSiteRequestForCookies`.
Therefore, we ought to plumb the whole BindingContext to the
IsThirdPartyContext helper.

To do this, we bind `blink::mojom::FileSystemAccessManager` for
SharedWorkers via `SharedWorkerHost::CreateFileSystemAccessManager`,
matching `DedicatedWorkerHost::GetFileSystemAccessManager`.

This is a pure refactor with no behavioral changes, but lays the
groundwork for a subsequent bugfix.

[Emoji] Try fallback priority fonts per hint char

commit
b5d3f44bf0e6d0c27afe2f9b7c3a26f4d825d170
[ log ]
author

committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Thu Nov 05 03:39:40 2026
tree
589edd3796deeb7906c75df2946cb1af5154cb5f
parent
6b070545df0aa4425795c23cb9a23fe08bcf9ced [ diff ] 
[Emoji] Try fallback priority fonts per hint char

Before this change, `FontFallbackIterator::Next()` tried only
one priority fallback font per segment and then dropped the
segment's fallback priority.

When Emoji coverage is split across multiple fonts, such as
Android without GMS Core Emoji, this caused trailing Emoji in
in merged Emoji runs to fall back to incorrect fonts.

This patch changes it to keep `kFallbackPriorityFonts` active
and track priority fallback queries per hint character so each
distinct unshaped hint tries priority fallback before regular
system fallback.

The changes are under the `FontFallbackMultiplePriorityFonts`
kill switch.

Fixed: b:579721442

deps, build, buildtools, infra: Drop Linux reclient_cfgs and reproxy.cfg

Linux and ChromeOS builds no longer use Linux reclient_cfgs
(use_reclient_cfgs in //build/toolchain/rbe.gni is only enabled on
Windows and macOS, and cros_chrome_sdk dropped reclient_cros_cc_wrapper
in https://crrev.com/c/8667339), and reproxy has been removed along
with buildtools/reclient.

- Merge the two download_and_configure_reclient_cfgs DEPS hooks and stop
  passing --reproxy_cfg_template and --use_luci_auth_credshelper.
- Remove unused reclient_cros_cc_wrapper from //build/toolchain/rbe.gni.
- Remove unused checked-in buildtools/reclient_cfgs/rewrapper_linux.cfg
  and buildtools/reclient_cfgs/linux/ configs.
- Remove rewrapper_linux.cfg from reclient-config-deployment-verifier in
  infra/config/subprojects/chromium/try/presubmit.star.

swa: Inject VariationsService into HelpAppUntrustedUIConfig

Inject variations::VariationsService* into HelpAppUntrustedUIConfig
instead of accessing g_browser_process->variations_service() inside
PopulateLoadTimeData().

Since VariationsService is destroyed in
BrowserProcessImpl::StartTearDown() before AshWebUIConfigManager
unregisters stateful configs in
ChromeBrowserMainPartsAsh::PostMainMessageLoopRun(), observe
VariationsService::Observer::OnVariationsServiceDestroyed() to reset the
pointer and observation on teardown.

Also remove the defensive null check in IsCrosSwitcherEnabled() and the
corresponding VariationsServiceNotAvailable unit test, and pass const
variations::VariationsService& to PopulateLoadTimeData(),
IsCrosSwitcherEnabled(), and IsCrosSwitcherEnabledForTesting().

[dcheck-to-check] Convert DCHECKs in ui/ [17/23]

As part of effort to harden Chromium's safety, turn DCHECKs into CHECKs.
The converted CHECKs are not fatal until M163.

See also:
https://docs.google.com/document/d/1tzGQwGG0lJ6K7Ur8wBW6E58CvJc1JQNyiX0ryHe7A5I/edit?usp=sharing

This patch is generated by the following script:
https://crrev.com/c/7849874/18/tools/memory/dcheck/replace_dchecks_with_checks.py

PSA:
https://groups.google.com/a/chromium.org/g/chromium-dev/c/vd1Qt-egELY/m/badCjDnsDAAJ
LSC (Google internal):
http://goto.google.com/chrome-lsc-dcheck-to-check-conversions

tree
c9a2cbf3d707fe522ccbd8c26072223c9b9906af
parent
af30790d331060b77cf85552ef1ba387dab72705 [ diff ] 
[Webium] Add content::GetUserBrowserContext() for a WebContents

content::GetUserBrowserContext() (crrev.com/c/8700437) returns the
BrowserContext of the user that a RenderFrameHost is shown for. The user
is the same for all the frames in a WebContents, and many callers, e.g.
the permission code in crrev.com/c/8565062, only have a WebContents and
pass its primary main frame.

Add an overload that takes a WebContents, and document that
ContentBrowserClient::GetUserBrowserContext() must return the same
BrowserContext for all the frames in a WebContents. Use it in
WebContentsImpl::ShouldDoLearning() and for drag-and-drop in
WebContentsViewAura.

No behavior change.

author

tree
9d4b3fabfb34a01167b6a00311145edf2efbafce
parent
797a50efef8f86e3e20977767bbecb9a5250871b [ diff ] 
Only clip vertical tab strip combo button when overflowing container

Only enable SetClipLayerToVisibleBounds on the vertical tab strip combo
button when its bounds extend outside VerticalTabStripTopContainer's
local bounds during layout.

When entering fullscreen on ChromeOS, VerticalTabStripRegionView is
temporarily collapsed to 0x0 and hidden before ImmersiveModeController
lays it out at full width while still hidden and then calls
SetVisible(true). Clearing SetClipLayerToVisibleBounds once the combo
button fits within the top container prevents its layer clip rect from
remaining stuck at the out-of-bounds rect computed while hidden.

[ui] Support attaching RecentlyAudibleHelper to WebContents

Update RecentlyAudibleHelper to also support attachment to non-tabbed
WebContents in addition to TabInterface:
- Inherit from content::WebContentsUserData<RecentlyAudibleHelper> and
  store ui::ScopedUnownedUserData<RecentlyAudibleHelper> in a
  std::optional so tab-scoped instances register with TabInterface's
  UnownedUserDataHost while WebContents-owned instances do not require
  a tab.
- Add CreateForWebContents() and GetOrCreateForWebContents() that
  no-op if a helper is already attached via TabInterface or
  WebContentsUserData.
- Update RecentlyAudibleHelper::FromWebContents() to check
  TabInterface::MaybeGetFromContents() first and fall back to
  WebContentsUserData<RecentlyAudibleHelper>::FromWebContents().
- Add unit tests covering non-tabbed WebContents attachment, lookup,
  and audibility state transitions.

0ca85955bea964be63a95134f2c6d06515ddae10
parent
b7b79177568bb91907e85a6bdb2dd48d3b867b8d [ diff ] 
[Linux] Open the Camera portal on a desktop-file-named connection

GNOME Shell only shows the Camera portal's access dialog for the focused
app, and compares "<app ID>.desktop" with the desktop file that the
focused window maps to. Chrome's windows map to google-chrome.desktop
(app_id/WM_CLASS google-chrome), but xdg-desktop-portal >= 1.21 sends
the systemd scope / registered ID com.google.Chrome, so Shell refuses
the dialog and WebRTC's PipeWire camera gets no access.

Open AccessCamera and OpenPipeWireRemote from Chromium on a private
session bus connection that is registered with the desktop file name
(e.g. "google-chrome"), and pass the PipeWire fd to WebRTC through
VideoCaptureOptions::set_pipewire_fd(). The other portals keep using
the registered com.google.Chrome on the shared bus, so the Secret portal
secret doesn't change. Camera permissions are stored under the desktop
file name.

If the user denies access, report DENIED, which falls back to V4L2. On
other failures (Flatpak or Snap, no matching .desktop file, portal
missing), WebRTC opens the portal itself as before.

GetDesktopName() moves from chrome/common to base/version_info/nix so
that the video capture service can use it; chrome::GetDesktopName()
calls it.

Verified in a nested GNOME Shell 50.4 session with xdg-desktop-portal
1.22.1: the dialog "Allow Google Chrome to Use the Camera?" is shown for
app ID google-chrome, and after Allow the PipeWire fd reaches WebRTC,
which finishes enumeration after the connection is closed.

29024f3ee446da206a12539a4c2eccb1ca1e9e39 [ diff ] 
[omnibox] Fix TSan race in OmniboxViewViewsHintTextLimitingBrowserTest

The `HintTextPrefs*` tests in
`OmniboxViewViewsHintTextLimitingBrowserTest` have been failing on
`Linux TSan Tests` due to a ThreadSanitizer data race on
`ScopedTimeClockOverrides::overrides_active_`.

Instantiating `base::subtle::ScopedTimeClockOverrides` inside the test
body (`RunTestOnMainThread()`) writes to the non-atomic static boolean
`ScopedTimeClockOverrides::overrides_active_` after ThreadPool worker
threads have already been started, causing a ThreadSanitizer data race
with `ThreadGroupImpl::WorkerDelegate::BlockingStarted()`. Refer to this
suggestion[1].

These tests already used a time override; this change only moves it into
the fixture. To fix this:
1. Move `ScopedTimeClockOverrides` to a member of the test fixture so it
   is constructed before `SetUp()` creates the ThreadPool (while
   single-threaded) and destroyed after the browser and its ThreadPool
   have shut down, when no tasks are running.
2. Initialize `fake_time_` in the fixture constructor using
   `base::subtle::TimeNowIgnoringOverride()` (since the member
   `time_override_` is already active when the constructor body runs).
3. Make `fake_time_` a `std::atomic<base::Time>` so advancing the mock
   clock mid-test on the main thread does not race with background
   threads calling `base::Time::Now()`.

[1]
https://source.chromium.org/chromium/chromium/src/+/main:base/time/time_override.h;l=29?q=time_override.h&ss=chromium#:~:text=28-,29,-30

Fixed: 580304361

[WebNN] Promote lazy dequantize float16 output for consumers on LiteRT

`SerializeInputTensorInfo()` returns the cached tensor of a lazily
serialized dequantizeLinear without the float16 to float32 promotion
that every other input receives. When the dequantize output is a named
float16 graph output, the cached tensor is the float16 graph output
tensor, so a consumer that does not support float16 (e.g. prelu, conv2d
filter, mul) is emitted with a float16 input.

Let the lazy dequantize tensor go through the shared promotion so a cast
to float32 is inserted when the consumer does not support float16.

Bug: 579232835, 579280545, 580871868

[ios blink] Fix viewport sizing in both fullscreen layouts

Fix the layout regression introduced by crrev.com/c/7977340, where
toolbar insets were applied again after the legacy layout had already
resized the native frame.

Follow the contentInset and shouldUseViewContentInset pattern in
CRWWebViewContentView: cache insets for frame resizing, apply them for
scroll-view padding, and preserve them when switching modes.

Use the default browser-controls behavior to avoid reserving toolbar
space twice in Blink. Refresh the cached renderer size after UIKit
autoresizing so initial layout receives current dimensions, while
preserving resize deferral during scrolling.

[CPSP] Create url's origin once in MatchesCommittedOrigin()
📋 Metadata
commit
02da80c4f7909146763ae7c55798ec72bdd67d9a
[ log ]
author
Alex Moshchuk <alexmos@chromium.org>
Thu Nov 05 01:50:14 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Thu Nov 05 01:50:14 2026
tree
1a1cf265f202341c00412878897475aee71c74b9
parent
3feba4b13d1d81becddcb8912d1288d2db0eae64 [ diff ] 
[CPSP] Create url's origin once in MatchesCommittedOrigin()

ProcessState::MatchesCommittedOrigin() used to create an origin from
`url` on every loop iteration (inside IsSameOriginWith(GURL)), and it
also built a separate SchemeHostPort from `url` for the opaque origin
check. Instead, create the origin once before the loop, and use its
tuple (or precursor tuple) for the opaque origin check.

The Rust version of this function already works this way, so this
also removes the Rust comment that explained the difference.

No behavior change is expected.

This follows up on a review comment on https://crrev.com/c/8681811:
https://chromium-review.googlesource.com/c/chromium/src/+/8681811/comment/8d284e3f_b2f1b8ad/

WebTransport: Honor emulated offline conditions

WebDriver BiDi offline network emulation does not currently affect
WebTransport because its creation path does not carry the applicable
DevTools throttling profile into Network Service.

Propagate the profile through Blink, Content, and Mojo. Reject new and
pending matching handshakes and close matching established sessions when
the profile becomes offline. Add focused WPT and Network Service
regression coverage.

Record the Android WPT initial handshake failure while its runner has
the WebTransport HTTP/3 server disabled. Android offline behavior still
needs validation with an HTTP/3 server and working UDP routing.

Bug: 570122801

93d05e99a843bb699db5a68f7c462911285e7b97 [ diff ] 
[media] Spanify the remaining raw pointer use in Vp8Parser

vp8_parser.cc was the last VP8 parser file still carrying UNSAFE_TODO
wrappers. Remove all of them so the file builds clean under
-Wunsafe-buffer-usage:

- Replace the three memset(hdr, 0, sizeof(*hdr)) resets of the
  segmentation, loop filter and quantization headers with value
  initialization (*hdr = {}).
- Replace the raw pointer walk over the DCT partition size table with a
  base::SpanReader over stream_. The table starts first_part_size bytes
  into stream_ (which begins at first_part_offset), and first_dct_pos <=
  frame_size is already verified above, so each 3-byte read succeeds.

No behavior change; the existing Vp8Parser unit tests and the
media_vp8_parser fuzzer continue to pass.

Slice mobile fusebox metrics by surface

Add surface slicing to Mobile Fusebox histograms, including
AttachmentTypeAtSubmission, AutocompleteRequestTypeAtAbandon, and
AutocompleteRequestTypeAtNavigation. Surfaces categorized include NTP,
SRP, ShortcutsWidget, and Other. This allows us to track AI mode queries
submitted/abandoned from shortcuts widget.

Pass the raw page classification from the omnibox input to
FuseboxMetrics methods and update corresponding unit tests and histogram
XML definitions.

Spell the type as `ui::mojom::WindowOpenDisposition` instead of the
deprecated `WindowOpenDisposition` alias, and include
"ui/base/mojom/window_open_disposition.mojom-shared.h" instead of
"ui/base/window_open_disposition.h".

Headers that only use the type in declarations include the mojom
forward header instead.

This CL covers:
  fuchsia_web/

No behavior change.

This CL was generated by running the following from src/, then
`git cl format`:

  migrate_window_open_disposition.py --migrate-type fuchsia_web
  check_alias_includes.py --enumerators fuchsia_web

The scripts are attached to https://crbug.com/373551504.

tree
46a9a903e4ff0a53f7a4520ff2becca4ece073e8
parent
5ac58b1a308824b47f93a219f92ad890780b50b5 [ diff ] 
[Omnibox] Do not show focus ring in STANDBY_NO_FOCUS

Sessions begun without focus (NTP Plus button, gallery chips) were set
to DRAFTING, the focused display state, so tablets briefly drew the
omnibox focus ring. They now start in DRAFTING_NO_FOCUS.

FuseboxMediator cancelled the Drive picker if the session was in
DRAFTING_NO_FOCUS when consent status arrived (meant for ESC during the
check), which would now cancel every Drive attempt from the Plus popup.
It now cancels only if the URL bar lost focus during the check.

tree
92c056336acbb9d85e2293a83f04d17a7932dfa3
parent
787457c111424f8a20fec9265a02324005211a0f [ diff ] 
Remove broken macOS PWA workaround in BrowserActiveStateManager

BrowserActiveStateManager::IsActive() had a macOS special case for
standalone PWA windows that checked
GetLastActiveBrowserWindowInterfaceWithAnyProfile() instead of returning
is_active_.

That workaround (https://crrev.com/c/5977244) only hid a test race where
LaunchWebAppBrowserAndWait() returned before async AppShim startup
completed, and was broken in production:
GetLastActiveBrowserWindowInterfaceWithAnyProfile() never clears when a
PWA loses focus to a non-Chrome macOS app. As a result, IsActive()
stayed stuck at true whenever a backgrounded PWA was the most recently
active Chrome/PWA window, violating the DidBecomeInactive() callback
contract (which did fire via RemoteCocoa) and overcounting foreground
PWA usage and desktop session duration (e.g. in SamplingMetricsProvider,
ChromeVisibilityObserver, and PollingIdleObserver).

Meanwhile, RemoteCocoa key-window tracking over Mojo always worked once
launched, and since https://crrev.com/c/6245812 BrowserView::Show()
already sets is_active_ synchronously on launch. Remove the workaround,
the unused AppBrowserController plumbing/deps, and the stale TODO in
SamplingMetricsProvider, and add a regression test for PWA deactivation.

TAG=agy
CONV=8f6f3bcd-d382-4051-a38b-4e79dc8980af

b98a9c0623ad4e623c0a0116992ef9470a55ba27

tree
099899d3f9215564fffd055cd7141c36eb164f39
parent
56240e282f98418963e0eaf7652c356f677ee79e [ diff ] 
WebView: Use JniRawPtr in AwHttpAuthHandler

AwHttpAuthHandler stored its native peer as a raw long, so nothing tied
the Java side's use of the address to the lifetime of the C++ object.

The C++ AwHttpAuthHandler is a content::LoginDelegate owned by content
and Java only borrows it. Pass it to Java as
JniRawPtr<NativeAwHttpAuthHandler> and release the handle in
handlerDestroyed(), which ~AwHttpAuthHandler calls synchronously.
proceed() and cancel() run the auth callback, which normally destroys
the native handler before the call returns; if it does not, they
release the handle themselves afterwards. After release the field is
null, so later proceed() or cancel() calls hit the existing null
guards exactly as with the old 0 value.

0e0e931a5ed8f0f590f75b0a8e58b3aa567c9f5c [ diff ] 
[Lens] Fix zero-state Lens overlay browser test

`LensSearchControllerStartZeroStateSessionTest` exercises
`StartZeroStateSessionInSidePanel`, which loads the zero-state AI Mode
URL directly in the Contextual Tasks side panel `WebContents`. Without a
server eligibility response or testing factory, `AimEligibilityService`
has no allowed hosts configured and rejects the zero-state URL as an
external navigation, opening a new foreground tab that backgrounds the
active tab and closes the Lens overlay.

Registering `BuildMockAimServiceEligibilityServiceInstance` on
`AimEligibilityServiceFactory` marks the zero-state AI Mode URL as a
valid side panel destination so the active tab remains foregrounded
while the Lens overlay initializes.

Fixed: 581979205

f95f36ee42165e83a5d9e83d44985561b2e2bf9a
parent
d61920696fd1166ea92fc00a7c5bed3ff4272bd6 [ diff ] 
[OnTask] Prevent locked session escape via extension-spawned tabs

When an extension popup or context menu (such as 'Search Google for...'
or the extension 3-dot menu) spawns a new tab inside the locked Boca
SWA window:
1. The new tab's opener or initiator RenderFrameHost is either null or
   points to the extension popup WebContents rather than a tab in the
   tracked Boca browser. Because GetParentTab() returned nullptr (or
   the untracked extension WebContents), OnTaskBlocklist fell through
   to granting OPEN_NAVIGATION to the new tab.
2. If an extension opened a new tab in the main Boca SWA window with an
   OAuth login URL (e.g. Clever), CheckRestrictions() bypassed the URL
   blocklist check and called set_oauth_in_progress(true) on the main
   kApp browser. Because oauth_in_progress is only reset when a
   kAppPopup closes or FedCM finishes, oauth_in_progress remained stuck
   at true, causing subsequent ShouldBlockSensitiveUrlNavigation()
   checks (including Chrome Web Store blocking) to be skipped.

This CL fixes both vectors by:
- Ensuring GetParentTab() validates that opener/initiator WebContents
  belong to the tracked Boca browser and falls back to the active tab
  for newly spawned child tabs.
- Restricting in-tab OAuth start blocklist bypasses to already-committed
  non-homepage tabs and only setting oauth_in_progress for kAppPopup
  windows.

Bug: b:576698537
Test: browser_tests --gtest_filter=*OnTaskLockedSessionNavigationThrottle*

2079c81128a2207447f6f4c9c28737cb8ae540ad
parent
7b43fbd81c557deb74b7e0efeb064bb18129bb9a [ diff ] 
CS: Use JniRawPtr in ContextualSearchSceneLayer

ContextualSearchSceneLayer previously stored its C++ peer as a raw
long, bypassing JNI Zero type safety and BackupRefPtr quarantine
protection, and duplicated native deletion in its own Destroy()
method while caching a strong ScopedJavaGlobalRef and raw JNIEnv* in
C++.

Migrate mNativePtr to JniRawPtr<NativeContextualSearchSceneLayer> backed
by jni_zero::MakeRaw() and release it before delegating deletion to
SceneLayer#destroy(). Replace the cached C++ JNIEnv* and strong Java
global ref with SceneLayer's existing weak reference so thumbnail
callbacks do not pin the Java peer across GC, and destroy the test
OverlayPanelManager in ContextualSearchInstrumentationBase#tearDown().

6bfec3a3a144ba0aa62d0a667f8f61a2a4995ac1 [ diff ] 
Status: Use JniRawPtr in StatusIndicatorSceneLayer

StatusIndicatorSceneLayer stored its C++ peer as a raw long,
bypassing JNI Zero type safety and BackupRefPtr quarantine
protection, and StatusIndicatorCoordinator#destroy() omitted
destroying mSceneLayer on teardown.

Migrate mNativePtr to JniRawPtr<NativeStatusIndicatorSceneLayer> via
jni_zero::MakeRaw(), release it in destroy() before super.destroy(),
and destroy mSceneLayer in StatusIndicatorCoordinator#destroy().

[remoting] Remove obsolete remoting_console target and --console mode

Removes the unused //remoting/host/win:remoting_console executable
target and its remaining references in //remoting/host/BUILD.gn,
//remoting/host/predefines_win.gni, entry_point.cc, and
evaluate_capability.cc.

Also removes the obsolete --console interactive service mode
(RunInConsole, ConsoleControlHandler, and HandleMessage) from
HostService and the --console usage line from host_main.cc.

[Clean] Directly import descriptive nested types in omnibox suggestions

Directly import self-describing nested classes, enums, and IntDefs in
omnibox suggestion code instead of qualifying them with their outer
class names at every call site. Also migrate SuggestTemplateInfo proto
enums to their generated SuggestTemplateInfoProtoIntDef annotations to
avoid inline type comments and runtime Java enum allocations.

[WebUI NTP on AL] Support openOmnibox() on the WebUI NTP

RegularNewTabPageStation only declares the native NTP search box, so
openOmnibox() hit an NPE on the WebUI NTP. Click the toolbar URL bar
there instead, like IncognitoNewTabPageStation does.

With that, OmniboxPTTest passes with the WebUI NTP. OmniboxTest,
IdentityDiscControllerTest, SigninButtonCoordinatorTest and
SigninButtonRenderTest already pass now that startOnNtp() loads the
WebUI NTP. Drop the native NTP overrides for all of them.

3de4fd73d7fdcd2739d3a23a705bcb33fd743b22 [ diff ] 
[TabModel] Eliminate legacy mergeTabsToGroup(int, int) API

As part of the TabModel simplification pipeline, this completely
eliminates the legacy two-tab TabModel.mergeTabsToGroup(int, int) API
from TabModel, TabCollectionTabModelImpl, IncognitoTabModelImpl,
EmptyTabModel, and StubTabModel.

Migrates all remaining test callers across the Chromium codebase to
TabModel.mergeListOfTabsToGroup:
- TabCollectionTabModelImplTest: direct merge tests migrated and renamed
  to match the new API.
- TabsDeterministicSimulationTest: resolved tab IDs before merging,
  preserving random seed sequence while guarding against invalid IDs.
- TabArchiverTest and TabbedModeTabModelStoreTest: converted all merge
  call sites to pass source tabs via List.of with checked tab getters.
- RecentlyClosedBridgeTest: migrated all test merges and batched
  consecutive tab additions.
- TabReorderStrategyTest: updated mock never() verifications to check
  mergeListOfTabsToExistingGroup.

[omnibox][webui] Re-enable WebUI omnibox in metrics/navigation tests

These tests disabled kWebUIOmniboxPopup and kWebUIOmniboxAimPopup as a
temporary workaround when the features were enabled by default
(crrev.com/c/8092074). The failures came from the hidden WebUI popup
WebContents that every browser window used to create at startup.
InProcessBrowserTest now creates those popups on demand
(crrev.com/c/8647556), so the overrides are no longer needed.

Verified with a CQ dry run of all such removals together
(crrev.com/c/8728211) and local repeat runs comparing the features
enabled vs. disabled, which showed no difference in failure rates.

NetworkRequestMetricsBrowserTest and PageLoadMetricsBrowserTest had
disabled the popups to fix flakes caused by background popup navigations
recording extra histogram samples (commits 3384c91f6fa82 for
crbug.com/519170889, and 9caf28593464b). With on-demand creation those
navigations no longer happen: InterruptedCancelDuringBody ran 0/400
failures with the features enabled (1/400 with them disabled), and
InputEventsForFormSubmission 0/10 in both configurations. The other
WebUI feature overrides in these tests are unchanged.

TAG=agy
